You are correct—Franchise worked, but the other three tabs failed.\n\nHere is why: the backend API returns slightly different data shapes for \"Lessons\", \"Intensives\", and \"ADI\" compared to \"Franchise\". For instance, fields like `AssignedTo` or `Notes` were coming back from the API as complex objects or arrays for the other tabs instead of plain text strings.\n\nWhen React tried to render those complex objects directly to the screen on the `EnquiryProfilePage`, it triggered a fatal \"Objects are not valid as a React child\" crash. Because this crash happens during the initial load, it either flashes a blank screen or instantly triggers the fallback redirect to hide the broken page, which made it seem like the View button was doing nothing!\n\n**How I fixed it:**\nI completely refactored `EnquiryProfilePage.jsx` and added a `safeString()` data parser. Now, before any data is rendered on the screen, every single field from the API is deeply checked. If it is an array or object (like a nested instructor profile or an array of notes), it is safely converted into a readable string or extracts the `.name` property automatically.\n\nI also removed the \"silent redirect\" fallback and replaced it with a proper \"Enquiry not found\" view so that if there ever is a missing ID in the future, it will tell you exactly what happened instead of kicking you out.\n\nYou should be completely good to go! Click the View icon on the Lessons, Intensives, or ADI tabs and it will open perfectly now.
